Meeting records [Transcript](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1WSQbj0D6QDZq0cOCRD4PfyV9sWtMDtPnUBM7opONp6Q/edit?usp=drive_web&tab=t.1ba70uvh0naj) 

### **Summary**

Roleplaying game session debrief covered game mechanics, tactical combat planning, and group survival strategies for upcoming challenges.

**Gameplay Mechanics And Logistics**  
Players refined character abilities and finalized the construction of a craft for survival. The group opted to participate in the upcoming bonus round challenge instead of evacuating.

**Combat And Evasion Tactics**  
Combat encounters with aquatic predators necessitated creative distraction strategies using makeshift slingshots and projectiles. Defensive maneuvers successfully allowed for a tactical aerial escape into the forest canopy.

**Narrative And System Review**  
The team analyzed environmental obstacles and updated character progress milestones within the campaign. Strategic adjustments to game tools ensured effective monitoring of ongoing survival events.

### **Decisions**

## Aligned

* **Horde base strategy established** The group decided to pivot the Seven Days to Die strategy to focus on a specialized funnel-based horde base design instead of continuing forward progress.

* **Transport strategy set to raft** The team decided to prioritize constructing a raft using barrels and ropes for transport to the forest over other travel methods.

* **Raft design and weaponry strategy established** The craft design strategy is set to utilize grappling hooks for dragging, fuel hoses for slingshots, bamboo reeds for blow darts, and fuel bladders with tar for the balloon.

* **Defensive warning shot strategy adopted** The defensive policy is set to utilize warning shots with slingshots, petrified seed pods, and root bundles as the primary deterrent against approaching parties instead of direct physical confrontation.

* **Eight AM positioning strategy finalized** The positioning strategy is set to station the raft 100 yards off-shore on the water at 8 AM.

* **Bonus round participation strategy confirmed** The participation strategy is set to continue activity through the bonus round beyond the 5 AM evacuation deadline.

* **Distraction target set to forest** The group decides that the shark distraction lure should be directed toward the forest area, rather than the center, to draw the threat away from their path.

* **Raft canopy positioning strategy** The group agrees to maneuver and tie off the raft within the tree canopy level to remain obscured while keeping the balloon inflated.

* **Ignatius renamed to Natty** The group agrees to refer to the character Ignatius as 'Natty' to avoid confusion with the existing character Iggy.

* **Raft construction strategy initiated** The team commits to constructing a raft utilizing barrels and bungee cords to navigate across the water to the Mazi forest.

* **Defensive weapon systems design finalized** The team integrates defensive systems into their craft, including slingshots utilizing petrified seed pods, root bundles, and bamboo reed blow darts.

* **Evacuation positioning and scouting protocol** The group decides to position themselves in the water at 8 AM, using Iggy for underwater scouting to detect potential threats.

* **Bonus round participation confirmed** The group commits to remaining for the bonus round after the 8 o'clock evacuation deadline.

* **Character level increase** All characters are advanced to level five for the next session.

We've **updated the Decisions section** using your feedback.

Holly Strebel opts to take the stress, establishing that Iggy now has a permanent phobia of trees as a result of the encounter.
